[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- **Dual render mode**: OSR (off-screen rendering with alpha transparency) and EmbeddedWindow (native child window, higher performance)
- **Inspector property grouping**: Browser Settings / Feature Toggles / Embedded Mode
- **Dynamic property visibility**: OSR mode hides embedded-mode properties, EmbeddedWindow hides OSR-specific properties
- **Cross-platform embedded window**: Windows (Win32), Linux (X11), macOS (Cocoa)
- **Keyboard event forwarding**: Forward browser keyboard events to Godot in EmbeddedWindow mode
- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x
- **JS ↔ C# bridge** via RegisterJavascriptObject (CEF IPC, no iframe needed)
- **GDScript bridge** via RegisterJsHandler (Callable-based, for GDExtension)

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

### C# (Plugin)

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
browser.Mode = RenderMode.OSR;        // OSR (transparent) or EmbeddedWindow
browser.Transparent = true;           // OSR only
AddChild(browser);
```

### GDScript (GDExtension)

```gdscript
var browser: CefGlueControl = $CefGlueControl
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org"
browser.FrameRate = 120
browser.Mode = 0  # 0=OSR, 1=EmbeddedWindow

# Connect to signals
browser.BrowserInitialized.connect(_on_ready)
browser.AddressChanged.connect(_on_address_changed)
browser.LoadStart.connect(_on_loading)
browser.LoadEnd.connect(_on_done)
browser.LoadError.connect(_on_error)
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture (Linux/macOS also supported)

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 149 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="149.7827.156" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="149.0.4" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official, not recommended — too old):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/                    ← This repository
│   ├── plugin/                   ← Godot .NET project (addon source + demo)
│   │   └── addons/GCefGlue/     ←    CefGlueControl C# scripts
│   ├── extension/                ← GDExtension C# project (AOT native lib)
│   │   └── Dll/                  ←    godot-dotnet bindings
│   ├── test/GDExtensionGame/     ← Godot project for testing GDExtension
│   ├── Nuget/                    ← Local NuGet packages (LFS)
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/                      ← Cloned CefGlue repository
```

## Project Structure

```
addons/GCefGlue/                    ← Plugin source code
├── CefGlueControl.cs               Skeleton (enum, fields, constructor)
├── CefGlueControl.Properties.cs    Export properties, static properties, events
├── CefGlueControl.Initialization.cs CEF init, lifecycle, browser creation
├── CefGlueControl.Rendering.cs     OSR paint, _Process, _Draw, cursor
├── CefGlueControl.Input.cs         Input forwarding, IME, _Notification
├── CefGlueControl.Bridge.cs        JS bridge, IPC, deserialization
├── CefGlueControl.Navigation.cs    Navigation, DevTools, CEF callbacks
├── CefGlueControl.Inspector.cs     Inspector property visibility (_ValidateProperty)
├── CefGlueControl.Events.cs        ForwardInputEvents event forwarding
├── CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs      Embedded window mode
├── CefInitializer.cs               CEF initialization
├── Handlers/                       CEF handlers
│   ├── GodotCefApp.cs
│   ├── GodotCefClient.cs
│   ├── GodotDisplayHandler.cs
│   ├── GodotLifeSpanHandler.cs
│   ├── GodotLoadHandler.cs
│   ├── GodotRenderHandler.cs
│   ├── GodotRequestHandler.cs
│   └── GodotBrowserProcessHandler.cs
└── Platform/                       Cross-platform native API
    ├── NativeWindowMethods.cs      Platform abstraction layer
    ├── X11Methods.cs               Linux X11 P/Invoke
    └── MacMethods.cs               macOS Cocoa P/Invoke
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.

2. **CEF Dependencies (Cross-Platform):**
   - **Windows:** Automatically obtained via NuGet package `chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64`
   - **Linux:** Requires [cef.redist.linux](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.linux) dependency
   - **macOS:** Requires [cef.redist.osx](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.osx) dependency

3. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

4. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.

5. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 149 | Maintained (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 149.7827.156` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| youfch/cef.redist.linux | 149 | Linux runtime | `cef.redist.linux64 149.0.4` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.linux) |
| youfch/cef.redist.osx | 149 | macOS runtime | `cef.redist.osx64 149.0.4` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.osx) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official (Legacy) | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Group | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | Browser Settings | URL to load when browser is created |
| `Mode` | RenderMode | OSR | Browser Settings | Render mode: OSR / EmbeddedWindow |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser Settings | Browser frame rate, 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | Browser Settings | Enable transparent background (OSR only) |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | Feature Toggles | Enable GPU hardware acceleration |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | Feature Toggles | Open popups in current browser |
| `SyncCursor` | bool | false | Feature Toggles | Sync cursor with web content (OSR only) |
| `ForwardInputEvents` | bool | false | Embedded Mode | Forward browser events to Godot (EmbeddedWindow only) |

### Dynamic Property Visibility

Properties automatically show/hide based on the selected `Mode`:

| Mode | Visible | Hidden |
|------|---------|--------|
| `OSR` | `SyncCursor`, `Transparent` | `ForwardInputEvents`, "Embedded Mode" group |
| `EmbeddedWindow` | `ForwardInputEvents`, "Embedded Mode" group | `SyncCursor` |

### RenderMode Enum

| Value | Description |
|-------|-------------|
| `OSR` (0) | Off-screen rendering. CEF renders to memory → Godot texture. **Supports transparency**. Cross-platform (Windows/Linux/macOS) |
| `EmbeddedWindow` (1) | Embedded native child window. CEF renders directly to OS window. **Better performance** (video/WebGL), **no transparency**. Cross-platform: Windows (Win32), Linux (X11), macOS (Cocoa) |

### ForwardInputEvents

When `Mode = EmbeddedWindow` and `ForwardInputEvents = true`, browser mouse/keyboard events are forwarded to Godot via IPC:

```
JS events → window.__godotEvents.forward(payload) → CEF IPC → C# → viewport.PushInput()
```

**Supported events:**

| Event | JS capture | Godot event |
|-------|-----------|-------------|
| `mouse_down` / `mouse_up` | `mousedown` / `mouseup` | `InputEventMouseButton` |
| `mouse_move` | `mousemove` | `InputEventMouseMotion` |
| `mouse_wheel` | `wheel` | `InputEventMouseButton(WheelUp/Down)` |
| `key_down` / `key_up` | `keydown` / `keyup` | `InputEventKey` |

**Coordinate mapping:** JS `clientX/clientY` (physical pixels) → Godot virtual pixels, respecting `ContentScale`.

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparency setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload page |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JS and return result (C# only) |
| `EvalJs(string code)` | Async JS eval, result via `eval_completed` signal (GDScript) |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open DevTools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close DevTools |

## JS ↔ C# Bridge

### C# (Plugin): RegisterJavascriptObject (Recommended) ✅

Register a C# object so JS can call its methods via V8 IPC (CEF SendProcessMessage).

**C# registration:**
```csharp
browser.RegisterJavascriptObject(new MyBridge(), "myBridge");
```

**JS call (returns Promise, use .then()):**
```javascript
window.myBridge.hello().then(function(result) {
    console.log(result);
}).catch(function(err) {
    console.error(err);
});
```

**C# → JS: evaluate and get result:**
```csharp
var title = await browser.EvaluateJavaScript<string>("document.title");
```

**C# → JS: push message:**
```csharp
browser.SendToJs("{\"type\":\"update\",\"payload\":{\"count\":42}}");
```

### GDScript (GDExtension): RegisterJsHandler

Register a GDScript Callable so JS can call it via V8 IPC.

**GDScript registration:**
```gdscript
browser.RegisterJsHandler("dotnetBridge", Callable(self, "_on_js_call"))
# Optional: specify method names (default: hello/echo/add/getVersion/eval)
browser.RegisterJsHandler("myBridge", my_handler, "[\"method1\",\"method2\"]")
```

**GDScript handler:**
```gdscript
func _on_js_call(method_name: String, args_json: String) -> Variant:
    match method_name:
        "hello":
            return "Hello from GDScript!"
        "add":
            var arr = JSON.parse_string(args_json) as Array
            return int(arr[0]) + int(arr[1])
```

**Async JS eval:**
```gdscript
browser.EvalJs("document.title")
# Result received via eval_completed signal:
browser.eval_completed.connect(_on_eval_result)
func _on_eval_result(result: String, error: String) -> void:
    print(result)
```

### API Overview

| API | Platform | Direction | Description | Status |
|-----|----------|-----------|-------------|--------|
| `RegisterJavascriptObject(target, name)` | Plugin | C# → JS | Register object callable from JS | ✅ Recommended |
| `RegisterJsHandler(name, Callable, methods)` | GDExtension | GDScript → JS | Register GDScript handler | ✅ Recommended |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(code, ...)` | Plugin | C# → JS | Execute JS and return result | ✅ C# only |
| `EvalJs(code)` | Both | GDScript/C# → JS | Async JS eval via signal | ✅ GDScript |
| `SendToJs(json)` | Both | C#/GDScript → JS | Push message to JS | ✅ Active |
| `SendResponse(cbId, json)` | Both | C#/GDScript → JS | Reply to bridge request | ✅ Active |
| `BridgeRequest` event | Both | JS → C# | Bridge request entry | ✅ Active |
| `bridge_request` signal | GDExtension | JS → GDScript | Bridge request signal | ✅ Active |

## CefGlueControl Signals

| Signal | Parameters | Description |
|--------|-----------|-------------|
| `BrowserInitialized` | — | Browser initialization complete |
| `AddressChanged` | `url: string` | Current page URL changed |
| `TitleChanged` | `title: string` | Page title changed |
| `LoadStart` | — | Page starts loading |
| `LoadEnd` | — | Page finishes loading |
| `LoadError` | `errorText, failedUrl` | Page failed to load |
| `eval_completed` | `result, error` | EvalJs result (GDExtension) |
| `bridge_request` | `type, payload, cbId` | Bridge request (GDExtension) |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed
1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs
1. Run `dotnet restore`
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page
1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
3. **Embedded window focus**: Clicking a Godot input after CEF may not release keyboard focus. Auto-handled via platform-specific APIs.
4. **JS Bridge S prefix**: CefGlue's serialization protocol prepends 'S' marker to strings. Automatically stripped.

49: browser.Mode = 0  # 0=OSR, 1=EmbeddedWindow
50: 
51: # Connect to signals
52: browser.BrowserInitialized.connect(_on_ready)
53: browser.AddressChanged.connect(_on_address_changed)
54: browser.LoadStart.connect(_on_loading)
55: browser.LoadEnd.connect(_on_done)
56: browser.LoadError.connect(_on_error)
57: ```
58: 
59: ## Requirements
60: 
61: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
62: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
63: - **Windows**: x64 architecture (Linux/macOS also supported)
64: 
65: ## Dependency Options
66: 
67: ### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)
68: 
69: The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.
70: 
71: **CEF 149 (Recommended):**
72: ```xml
73: <ItemGroup>
74:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="149.7827.156" />
75:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="149.0.4" />
76: </ItemGroup>
77: ```
78: 
79: **CEF 120 (Official, not recommended — too old):**
80: ```xml
81: <ItemGroup>
82:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
83:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
84: </ItemGroup>
85: ```
86: 
87: ### Option 2: Build from Source
88: 
89: If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:
90: 
91: ```bash
92: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
93: ```
94: 
95: Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
96: ```
97: YourProject/
98: ├── GDCefGlue/                    ← This repository
99: │   ├── plugin/                   ← Godot .NET project (addon source + demo)
100: │   │   └── addons/GCefGlue/     ←    CefGlueControl C# scripts
101: │   ├── extension/                ← GDExtension C# project (AOT native lib)
102: │   │   └── Dll/                  ←    godot-dotnet bindings
103: │   ├── test/GDExtensionGame/     ← Godot project for testing GDExtension
104: │   ├── Nuget/                    ← Local NuGet packages (LFS)
105: │   ├── img/
106: │   └── README*.md
107: └── CefGlue/                      ← Cloned CefGlue repository
108: ```
109: 
110: ## Project Structure
111: 
112: ```
113: addons/GCefGlue/                    ← Plugin source code
114: ├── CefGlueControl.cs               Skeleton (enum, fields, constructor)
115: ├── CefGlueControl.Properties.cs    Export properties, static properties, events
116: ├── CefGlueControl.Initialization.cs CEF init, lifecycle, browser creation
117: ├── CefGlueControl.Rendering.cs     OSR paint, _Process, _Draw, cursor
118: ├── CefGlueControl.Input.cs         Input forwarding, IME, _Notification
119: ├── CefGlueControl.Bridge.cs        JS bridge, IPC, deserialization
120: ├── CefGlueControl.Navigation.cs    Navigation, DevTools, CEF callbacks
121: ├── CefGlueControl.Inspector.cs     Inspector property visibility
122: ├── CefGlueControl.Events.cs        ForwardInputEvents event forwarding
123: ├── CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs      Embedded window mode
124: ├── CefInitializer.cs               CEF initialization
125: ├── Handlers/                       CEF handlers
126: │   ├── GodotCefApp.cs
127: │   ├── GodotCefClient.cs
128: │   ├── GodotDisplayHandler.cs
129: │   ├── GodotLifeSpanHandler.cs
130: │   ├── GodotLoadHandler.cs
131: │   ├── GodotRenderHandler.cs
132: │   ├── GodotRequestHandler.cs
133: │   └── GodotBrowserProcessHandler.cs
134: └── Platform/                       Cross-platform native API
135:     ├── NativeWindowMethods.cs      Platform abstraction layer
136:     ├── X11Methods.cs               Linux X11 P/Invoke
137:     └── MacMethods.cs               macOS Cocoa P/Invoke
138: ```
139: 
140: ## Project Types
141: 
142: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
143: 
144: Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
145: 
146: **Features:**
147: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
148: - Compiled as native AOT library
149: - Better performance and smaller file size
150: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
151: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
152: 
153: **Structure:**
154: - `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
155: - `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
156: - `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing
157: 
158: **Build Instructions:**
159: 
160: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
161:    
162:    Different Godot versions require corresponding godot-dotnet versions:
163:    
164:    | Godot Version | godot-dotnet Branch |
165:    |---------------|---------------------|
166:    | 4.6.x         | master or corresponding tag |
167:    | 4.5.x         | Check corresponding release tag |
168:    | 4.4.x         | Check corresponding release tag |
169:    
170:    > **Note:** godot-dotnet does not publish release packages. You need to check the commit history, download the source code for your Godot version, and compile manually.
171:    
172:    ```bash
173:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
174:    cd godot-dotnet
175:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
176:    ```
177:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.
178: 
179: 2. **CEF Dependencies (Cross-Platform):**
180:    
181:    - **Windows:** Automatically obtained via NuGet package `chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64`
182:    - **Linux:** Requires [cef.redist.linux](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.linux) dependency
183:    - **macOS:** Requires [cef.redist.osx](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.osx) dependency
184:    
185:    See [CefGlue repository](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) for how to add cross-platform dependencies.
186: 
187: 3. **Build GDExtension:**
188:    
189:    Navigate to `extension` directory and run:
190:    
191:    **Windows x64:**
192:    ```bash
193:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
194:    dotnet publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true
195:    ```
196:    
197:    **Linux x64:**
198:    ```bash
199:    dotnet publish -c Release -r linux-x64 --self-contained true
200:    ```
201:    
202:    **macOS x64/ARM64:**
203:    ```bash
204:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-x64 --self-contained true
205:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-arm64 --self-contained true
206:    ```
207: 
208: 4. **Deploy:**
209:    Build output is located at `bin\Release(Debug)\net10.0\win-x64\publish\` (Windows)
210:    Copy all files from the publish directory to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.
211: 
212: 5. **Run:**
213:    Open `test/GDExtensionGame/` project with Godot 4.6 and run.
214: 
215: ### Normal C# Project
216: 
217: Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.
218: 
219: **Features:**
220: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
221: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
222: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
223: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`
224: 
225: Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
226: ```xml
227: <ItemGroup>
228:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
229:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
230:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
231: </ItemGroup>
232: ```
233: 
234: ### CefGlue Sources
235: 
236: | Source | CEF Version | Status | Link |
237: |--------|-------------|--------|------|
238: | youfch/CefGlue | 149 | Maintained (Recommended) | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
239: | youfch/cef.redist.linux | 149 | Linux runtime | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.linux) |
240: | youfch/cef.redist.osx | 149 | macOS runtime | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.osx) |
241: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official (Legacy) | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
242: 
243: ## Build Instructions
244: 
245: ### Using NuGet Package
246: 
247: ```powershell
248: dotnet restore
249: dotnet build
250: ```
251: 
252: ### Using Source Code
253: 
254: ```powershell
255: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
256: dotnet restore
257: dotnet build
258: ```
259: 
260: ## Build Output
261: 
262: **Core Files:**
263: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
264: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
265: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
266: 
267: **CEF Native Files:**
268: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
269: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
270: - `resources.pak` - Application resources
271: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
272: 
273: **BrowserProcess Files:**
274: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files
275: 
276: ## Export for Distribution
277: 
278: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
279: 
280: ## CefGlueControl Properties
281: 
282: | Property | Type | Default | Group | Description |
283: |----------|------|---------|-------|-------------|
284: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | Browser Settings | URL to load when browser is created |
285: | `Mode` | RenderMode | OSR | Browser Settings | Render mode: OSR / EmbeddedWindow |
286: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser Settings | Browser frame rate, 1-360 |
287: | `Transparent` | bool | false | Browser Settings | Enable transparent background (OSR only) |
288: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | Feature Toggles | Enable GPU hardware acceleration |
289: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | Feature Toggles | Open popups in current browser |
290: | `SyncCursor` | bool | false | Feature Toggles | Sync cursor with web content (OSR only) |
291: | `ForwardInputEvents` | bool | false | Embedded Mode | Forward browser events to Godot (EmbeddedWindow only) |
292: 
293: ### Dynamic Property Visibility
294: 
295: Properties automatically show/hide based on the selected `Mode`:
296: 
297: | Mode | Visible | Hidden |
298: |------|---------|--------|
299: | `OSR` | `SyncCursor`, `Transparent` | `ForwardInputEvents`, "Embedded Mode" group |
300: | `EmbeddedWindow` | `ForwardInputEvents`, "Embedded Mode" group | `SyncCursor` |
301: 
302: ### RenderMode Enum
303: 
304: | Value | Description |
305: |-------|-------------|
306: | `OSR` (0) | Off-screen rendering. CEF renders to memory → Godot texture. **Supports transparency**. Cross-platform (Windows/Linux/macOS) |
307: | `EmbeddedWindow` (1) | Embedded native child window. CEF renders directly to OS window. **Better performance** (video/WebGL), **no transparency**. Cross-platform: Windows (Win32), Linux (X11), macOS (Cocoa) |
308: 
309: ### ForwardInputEvents
310: 
311: When `Mode = EmbeddedWindow` and `ForwardInputEvents = true`, browser mouse/keyboard events are forwarded to Godot via IPC:
312: 
313: ```
314: JS events → window.__godotEvents.forward(payload) → CEF IPC → C# → viewport.PushInput()
315: ```
316: 
317: **Supported events:**
318: 
319: | Event | JS capture | Godot event |
320: |-------|-----------|-------------|
321: | `mouse_down` / `mouse_up` | `mousedown` / `mouseup` | `InputEventMouseButton` |
322: | `mouse_move` | `mousemove` | `InputEventMouseMotion` |
323: | `mouse_wheel` | `wheel` | `InputEventMouseButton(WheelUp/Down)` |
324: | `key_down` / `key_up` | `keydown` / `keyup` | `InputEventKey` |
325: 
326: **Coordinate mapping:** JS `clientX/clientY` (physical pixels) → Godot virtual pixels, respecting `ContentScale`.
327: 
328: ### Static Properties
329: 
330: | Property | Type | Description |
331: |----------|------|-------------|
332: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration, must be set before CEF initialization |
333: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparency setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
334: 
335: ### Read-only Properties
336: 
337: | Property | Type | Description |
338: |----------|------|-------------|
339: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
340: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
341: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
342: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
343: 
344: ## CefGlueControl Methods
345: 
346: | Method | Description |
347: |--------|-------------|
348: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back |
349: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward |
350: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to URL |
351: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload page |
352: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript |
353: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JS and return result |
354: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open DevTools |
355: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close DevTools |
356: 
357: ## JS ↔ C# Bridge
358: 
359: ### Method 1: RegisterJavascriptObject (Recommended) ✅
360: 
361: **C# registration:**
362: ```csharp
363: browser.RegisterJavascriptObject(new MyBridge(), "myBridge");
364: ```
365: 
366: **JS call (returns Promise, use .then()):**
367: ```javascript
368: window.myBridge.hello().then(function(result) {
369:     console.log(result);
370: }).catch(function(err) {
371:     console.error(err);
372: });
373: ```
374: 
375: **C# → JS: evaluate and get result:**
376: ```csharp
377: var title = await browser.EvaluateJavaScript<string>("document.title");
378: ```
379: 
380: **C# → JS: push message:**
381: ```csharp
382: browser.SendToJs("{\"type\":\"update\",\"payload\":{\"count\":42}}");
383: ```
384: 
385: ### API Overview
386: 
387: | API | Direction | Description | Status |
388: |-----|-----------|-------------|--------|
389: | `RegisterJavascriptObject(target, name)` | C# → JS | Register object callable from JS | ✅ Recommended |
390: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(code, ...)` | C# → JS | Execute JS and return result | ✅ Recommended |
391: | `SendToJs(json)` | C# → JS | Push message to JS | ✅ Active |
392: | `SendResponse(cbId, json)` | C# → JS | Reply to legacy iframe request | 🔴 Deprecated |
393: | `BridgeRequest` event | JS → C# | Legacy iframe bridge entry | 🔴 Deprecated |
394: 
395: ## CefGlueControl Signals
396: 
397: | Signal | Parameters | Description |
398: |--------|-----------|-------------|
399: | `BrowserInitialized` | — | Browser initialization complete |
400: | `AddressChanged` | `url: string` | URL changed |
401: | `TitleChanged` | `title: string` | Title changed |
402: | `LoadStart` | — | Page starts loading |
403: | `LoadEnd` | — | Page finishes loading |
404: | `LoadError` | `errorText, failedUrl` | Page failed to load |
405: 
406: ## Troubleshooting
407: 
408: ### GPU Process Crashed
409: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
410: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration
411: 
412: ### Missing DLLs
413: 1. Run `dotnet restore`
414: 2. Clean and rebuild
415: 
416: ### Blank Page
417: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
418: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
419: 
420: ## Known Issues
421: 
422: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
423: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
424: 3. **Embedded window focus**: Clicking Godot input after CEF may not release keyboard focus. Auto-handled via platform-specific APIs.
425: 4. **JS Bridge S prefix**: CefGlue's serialization prepends 'S' marker to strings. Automatically stripped.
426:

149:

152:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 page_load_started
153:         /// </summary>
154:         [Signal]
155:         public delegate void PageLoadStartedEventHandler(string url);
156: 
157:         /// <summary>
158:         /// 页面加载完成
159:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 page_load_finished
160:         /// </summary>
161:         [Signal]
162:         public delegate void PageLoadFinishedEventHandler(string url);
163: 
164:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
165:         //  生命周期
166:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
167: 
168:         public override void _Ready()
169:         {
170:             // 编辑器模式下不创建 WebView，避免嵌入窗口和初始化异常
171:             if (Engine.IsEditorHint())
172:                 return;
173: 
174:             _isReady = true;
175:             InitializeWebView();
176:         }
177: 
178:         public override void _ExitTree()
179:         {
180:             CleanupWebView();
181:         }
182: 
183:         public override void _Process(double delta)
184:         {
185:             if (Engine.IsEditorHint())
186:                 return;
187: 
188:             if (_webview == null || !_webview.IsCreated)
189:                 return;
190: 
191:             SyncPosition();
192: 
193:             // Linux GTK 事件泵浦（参考 godot_wry）
194:             if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux() && _webview is IDisposable)
195:             {
196:                 try
197:                 {
198:                     // 通过反射调用 PumpGtkEvents（避免编译时依赖 LINUX 条件编译）
199:                     var method = _webview.GetType().GetMethod("PumpGtkEvents");
200:                     method?.Invoke(_webview, null);
201:                 }
202:                 catch { }
203:             }
204:         }
205: 
206:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
207:         //  初始化 / 清理
208:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
209: 
210:         private async void InitializeWebView()
211:         {
212:             try
213:             {
214:                 if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
215:                 {
216: #if MACOS || MAC
217:                     _webview = new MacWebViewBackend();
218: #else
219:                     GD.PrintErr("[WebViewSharp] macOS backend not compiled. Rebuild with target macOS.");
220:                     return;
221: #endif
222:                 }
223:                 else if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux())
224:                 {
225: #if LINUX
226:                     _webview = new LinuxWebViewBackend();
227: #else
228:                     GD.PrintErr("[WebViewSharp] Linux backend not compiled. Rebuild with target Linux.");
229:                     return;
230: #endif
231:                 }
232:                 else
233:                 {
234:                     _webview = new WindowsWebViewBackend();
235:                 }
236: 
237:                 await _webview.CreateAsync(this);
238: 
239:                 if (!_webview.IsCreated)
240:                 {
241:                     GD.PrintErr("[WebViewSharp] Failed to create webview");
242:                     return;
243:                 }
244: 
245:                 // 加载 URL 或 HTML
246:                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Url))
247:                     _webview.Navigate(ResolveUrl(Url));
248:                 else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Html))
249:                     _webview.NavigateToString(Html);
250: 
251:                 // 连接事件
252:                 _webview.NavigationStarting += OnNavigationStarting;
253:                 _webview.NavigationCompleted += OnNavigationCompleted;
254:                 _webview.WebMessageReceived += OnWebMessageReceived;
255:                 _webview.SourceChanged += OnSourceChanged;
256: 
257:                 // 初始同步
258:                 SyncPosition();
259: 
260:                 GD.Print($"[WebViewSharp] WebView initialized, Url={Url}");
261:             }
262:             catch (Exception ex)
263:             {
264:                 GD.PrintErr($"[WebViewSharp] WebView initialization failed: {ex.Message}");
265:             }
266:         }
267: 
268:         private void CleanupWebView()
269:         {
270:             if (_webview != null)
271:             {
272:                 _webview.Dispose();
273:                 _webview = null;
274:             }
275:         }
276: 
277:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
278:         //  位置同步（参考 godot_wry update_webview）
279:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
280: 
281:         private void SyncPosition()
282:         {
283:             if (_webview == null || !_webview.IsCreated)
284:                 return;
285: 
286:             var globalPos = GlobalPosition;
287:             var size = Size;
288:             if (size.X <= 0 || size.Y <= 0 && !FullWindowSize)
289:                 return;
290: 
291:             float contentScale = DisplayServer.ScreenGetScale();
292:             var windowPos = FullWindowSize
293:                 ? Vector2I.Zero
294:                 : DisplayServer.WindowGetPosition();
295: 
296:             // 仅在有变化时更新，避免不必要的开销
297:             if (globalPos == _previousGlobalPos
298:                 && size == _previousSize
299:                 && windowPos == _previousWindowPos
300:                 && Math.Abs(contentScale - _previousContentScale) < 0.001f)
301:             {
302:                 return;
303:             }
304: 
305:             _previousGlobalPos = globalPos;
306:             _previousSize = size;
307:             _previousWindowPos = windowPos;
308:             _previousContentScale = contentScale;
309: 
310:             int physX, physY, physW, physH;
311: 
312:             if (FullWindowSize)
313:             {
314:                 var windowSize = DisplayServer.WindowGetSize();
315:                 physX = 0;
316:                 physY = 0;
317:                 physW = (int)(windowSize.X * contentScale);
318:                 physH = (int)(windowSize.Y * contentScale);
319:             }
320:             else
321:             {
322:                 physX = (int)(globalPos.X * contentScale);
323:                 physY = (int)(globalPos.Y * contentScale);
324:                 physW = (int)(size.X * contentScale);
325:                 physH = (int)(size.Y * contentScale);
326:             }
327: 
328:             _webview.SetBounds(physX, physY, physW, physH);
329:         }
330: 
331:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
332:         //  事件处理
333:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
334: 
335:         private void OnNavigationStarting(string url)
336:         {
337:             IsLoading = true;
338:             CallDeferred(nameof(EmitPageLoadStarted), url);
339:         }
340: 
341:         private void OnNavigationCompleted(bool success, int httpStatusCode)
342:         {
343:             IsLoading = false;
344:             CallDeferred(nameof(EmitPageLoadFinished), CurrentUrl);
345:         }
346: 
347:         private void OnSourceChanged(string url)
348:         {
349:             CurrentUrl = url;
350:         }
351: 
352:         private void OnWebMessageReceived(string message)
353:         {
354:             if (ForwardInputEvents && InputEventRouter.TryProcessInputEvent(message, this))
355:                 return;
356: 
357:             EmitSignal(SignalName.IpcMessage, message);
358:         }
359: 
360:         private void EmitPageLoadStarted(string url)
361:         {
362:             EmitSignal(SignalName.PageLoadStarted, url);
363:         }
364: 
365:         private void EmitPageLoadFinished(string url)
366:         {
367:             EmitSignal(SignalName.PageLoadFinished, url);
368:         }
369: 
370:         private void EmitIpcMessage(string message)
371:         {
372:             EmitSignal(SignalName.IpcMessage, message);
373:         }
374: 
375:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
376:         //  公开 API（对标 godot_wry 的 func 方法）
377:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
378: 
379:         /// <summary>
380:         /// 向网页发送消息（触发 JS 的 message 事件）
381:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 post_message
382:         /// </summary>
383:         public void PostMessage(string message)
384:         {
385:             if (_webview?.IsCreated == true)
386:             {
387:                 string script = $"document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('message', {{ detail: {Json.Stringify(message)} }}))";
388:                 _webview.ExecuteScript(script);
389:             }
390:         }
391: 
392:         /// <summary>
393:         /// 在网页中执行 JavaScript
394:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 eval
395:         /// </summary>
396:         public void EvaluateJavaScript(string script)
397:         {
398:             _webview?.ExecuteScript(script);
399:         }
400: 
401:         /// <summary>
402:         /// 加载 URL
403:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 load_url
404:         /// </summary>
405:         public void LoadUrl(string url)
406:         {
407:             if (_webview?.IsCreated == true)
408:             {
409:                 string resolvedUrl = ResolveUrl(url);
410:                 _webview.Navigate(resolvedUrl);
411:             }
412:         }
413: 
414:         /// <summary>
415:         /// 加载 HTML 字符串
416:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 load_html
417:         /// </summary>
418:         public void LoadHtml(string html)
419:         {
420:             _webview?.NavigateToString(html);
421:         }
422: 
423:         /// <summary>
424:         /// 重新加载当前页面
425:         /// 对标 godot_wry 的 reload
426:         /// </summary>
427:         public void Reload()
428:         {
429:             _webview?.Reload();
430:         }
431: 
432:         /// <summary>
433:         /// 后退
434:         /// </summary>
435:         public void GoBack()
436:         {
437:             _webview?.GoBack();
438:         }
439: 
440:         /// <summary>
441:         /// 前进
442:         /// </summary>
443:         public void GoForward()
444:         {
445:             _webview?.GoForward();
446:         }
447: 
448:         /// <summary>
449:         /// 打开 DevTools
450:         /// </summary>
451:         public void OpenDevTools()
452:         {
453:             _webview?.OpenDevTools();
454:         }
455: 
456:         /// <summary>
457:         /// 关闭 DevTools
458:         /// </summary>
459:         public void CloseDevTools()
460:         {
461:             _webview?.CloseDevTools();
462:         }
463: 
464:         /// <summary>
465:         /// 清除所有浏览数据
466:         /// </summary>
467:         public void ClearAllBrowsingData()
468:         {
469:             _webview?.ClearBrowsingData();
470:         }
471: 
472:         /// <summary>
473:         /// 获取焦点
474:         /// </summary>
475:         public void FocusWebView()
476:         {
477:             _webview?.Focus();
478:         }
479: 
480:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
481:         //  辅助方法
482:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
483: 
484:         private string ResolveUrl(string url)
485:         {
486:             // res:// → https://godot.local/ 虚拟主机映射
487:             // 避免 file:// 协议下 ES Module 被 CORS 拦截
488:             if (url.StartsWith("res://"))
489:             {
490:                 string path = url.Substring(6).Replace("\\", "/");
491:                 return $"https://godot.local/{path}";
492:             }
493: 
494:             // user:// 协议
495:             if (url.StartsWith("user://"))
496:             {
497:                 string path = url.Substring(7).Replace("\\", "/");
498:                 string globalPath = ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath($"user://{path}");
499:                 return $"file:///{globalPath.Replace("\\", "/")}";
500:             }
501: 
502:             return url;
503:         }
504:     }
505: }

(End of file - total 505 lines)
</content>

8: namespace WebViewSharp

10:     /// <summary>

37:         private const int WS_EX_TRANSPARENT = 0x00000020;

43:         // ── WebView2 ──

45:         private CoreWebView2Environment _environment;
46:         private IntPtr _parentHwnd;
47:         private IntPtr _childHwnd;

50:         // ── 事件 ──
51:         public event Action<string> NavigationStarting;
52:         public event Action<bool, int> NavigationCompleted;
53:         public event Action<string> WebMessageReceived;
54:         public event Action<string> SourceChanged;

56:         public bool IsCreated => _controller != null;
57:         public bool CanGoBack => _controller?.CoreWebView2?.CanGoBack ?? false;
58:         public bool CanGoForward => _controller?.CoreWebView2?.CanGoForward ?? false;

60:         // ── JS 注入脚本（输入转发，对标 godot_wry 的 forward_input_events） ──
61:         private const string InputForwardingScript = @"
62: (function() {
63:     if (window.__webviewSharpInputForwardingInstalled) return;
64:     window.__webviewSharpInputForwardingInstalled = true;
65: 
66:     function postToGodot(data) {
67:         window.chrome.webview.postMessage(JSON.stringify(data));
68:     }
69: 
70:     document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
71:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
72:         postToGodot({
73:             type: '_mouse_move',
74:             x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
75:             movementX: e.movementX, movementY: e.movementY,
76:             button: e.button
77:         });
78:     });
79: 
80:     document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
81:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
82:         postToGodot({
83:             type: '_mouse_down', x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, button: e.button
84:         });
85:     });
86: 
87:     document.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
88:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
89:         postToGodot({
90:             type: '_mouse_up', x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, button: e.button
91:         });
92:     });
93: 
94:     document.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
95:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
96:         postToGodot({
97:             type: '_mouse_wheel', x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
98:             deltaX: e.deltaX, deltaY: e.deltaY,
99:             shift: e.shiftKey, ctrl: e.ctrlKey, alt: e.altKey, meta: e.metaKey
100:         });
101:     });
102: 
103:     document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
104:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
105:         const isModifier = ['Alt', 'Shift', 'Control', 'Meta'].includes(e.key);
106:         postToGodot({
107:             type: '_key_down', key: e.key, code: e.code, keyCode: e.keyCode,
108:             shift: isModifier ? false : e.shiftKey,
109:             ctrl: isModifier ? false : e.ctrlKey,
110:             alt: isModifier ? false : e.altKey,
111:             meta: isModifier ? false : e.metaKey
112:         });
113:     });
114: 
115:     document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
116:         if (!document.hasFocus()) return;
117:         const isModifier = ['Alt', 'Shift', 'Control', 'Meta'].includes(e.key);
118:         postToGodot({
119:             type: '_key_up', key: e.key, code: e.code, keyCode: e.keyCode,
120:             shift: isModifier ? false : e.shiftKey,
121:             ctrl: isModifier ? false : e.ctrlKey,
122:             alt: isModifier ? false : e.altKey,
123:             meta: isModifier ? false : e.metaKey
124:         });
125:     });
126: })();";
127: 
128:         public async Task CreateAsync(Control hostControl)
129:         {
130:             _host = hostControl as WebViewControl;
131: 
132:             try
133:             {
134:                 // 1. 获取 Godot 窗口 HWND
135:                 _parentHwnd = GetGodotWindowHandle();
136:                 if (_parentHwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
137:                 {
138:                     GD.PrintErr("[WebViewSharp] Failed to get Godot window handle");
139:                     return;
140:                 }
141: 
142:                 // 2. 移除 WS_CLIPCHILDREN（参考 godot_wry），否则透明背景不工作
143:                 int currentStyle = GetWindowLong(_parentHwnd, GWL_STYLE);
144:                 if ((currentStyle & WS_CLIPCHILDREN) != 0)
145:                 {
146:                     SetWindowLong(_parentHwnd, GWL_STYLE, currentStyle & ~WS_CLIPCHILDREN);
147:                 }
148: 
149: // 3. 创建 WebView2 环境
150:             var envOptions = new CoreWebView2EnvironmentOptions
151:             {
152:                 // 允许 file:// 加载 ES Module
153:                 AdditionalBrowserArguments = "--allow-file-access-from-files --disable-web-security"
154:             };
155: 
156:             string userDataDir = GetUserDataDirectory();
157: 
158:                 _environment = await CoreWebView2Environment.CreateAsync(
159:                     browserExecutableFolder: null,
160:                     userDataFolder: userDataDir,
161:                     options: envOptions
162:                 );
163: 
164:                 // 4. 创建 WebView2 控制器
165:                 _controller = await _environment.CreateCoreWebView2ControllerAsync(_parentHwnd);
166: 
167:                 if (_controller == null)
168:                 {
169:                     GD.PrintErr("[WebViewSharp] Failed to create WebView2 controller");
170:                     return;
171:                 }
172: 
173:                 // 5. 配置
174:                 ConfigureWebView2();
175: 
176:                 // 6. 将 WebView2 子窗口挂载到 Godot 窗口
177:                 _childHwnd = FindWebView2ChildHwnd();
178:                 if (_childHwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
179:                 {
180:                     SetParent(_childHwnd, _parentHwnd);
181:                 }
182: 
183:                 // 7. 注入脚本
184:                 if (_host?.ForwardInputEvents == true)
185:                 {
186:                     await _controller.CoreWebView2.AddScriptToExecuteOnDocumentCreatedAsync(InputForwardingScript);
187:                 }
188: 
189:                 // 注入 WRY 兼容层：window.ipc.postMessage → chrome.webview.postMessage
190:                 await _controller.CoreWebView2.AddScriptToExecuteOnDocumentCreatedAsync(@"
191:                     (function() {
192:                         if (!window.ipc) window.ipc = {};
193:                         if (!window.ipc.postMessage) {
194:                             window.ipc.postMessage = function(msg) {
195:                                 try {
196:                                     var wv = window.chrome && window.chrome.webview;
197:                                     if (wv && wv.postMessage) {
198:                                         wv.postMessage(typeof msg === 'string' ? msg : JSON.stringify(msg));
199:                                     }
200:                                 } catch(e) {}
201:                             };
202:                         }
203:                     })();
204:                 ");
205:                 _controller.CoreWebView2.Settings.IsWebMessageEnabled = true;
206: 
207:                 // 8. 设置初始背景
208:                 _controller.DefaultBackgroundColor = System.Drawing.Color.White;
209: 
210:                 // 9. 连接事件
211:                 _controller.CoreWebView2.NavigationStarting += OnNavigationStarting;
212:                 _controller.CoreWebView2.NavigationCompleted += OnNavigationCompleted;
213:                 _controller.CoreWebView2.SourceChanged += OnSourceChanged;
214:                 _controller.CoreWebView2.WebMessageReceived += OnWebMessageReceived;
215: 
216:                 // 10. 设置虚拟主机映射：godot.local → Godot 项目根目录
217:                 // 这样 ES Module 通过 HTTPS 加载，不被 CORS 拦截
218:                 try
219:                 {
220:                     string projectPath = ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath("res://");
221:                     _controller.CoreWebView2.SetVirtualHostNameToFolderMapping(
222:                         "godot.local", projectPath,
223:                         CoreWebView2HostResourceAccessKind.Allow);
224:                     GD.Print($"[WebViewSharp] Virtual host mapping: godot.local -> {projectPath}");
225:                 }
226:                 catch (Exception ex)
227:                 {
228:                     GD.PrintErr($"[WebViewSharp] Virtual host mapping failed: {ex.Message}");
229:                 }
230: 
231:                 // 11. 透明背景（对标 godot_wry 的 transparent 属性）
232:                 if (_host?.Transparent == true)
233:                     SetTransparent(true);
234: 
235:                 GD.Print("[WebViewSharp] WebView2 created");
236:             }
237:             catch (Exception ex)
238:             {
239:                 GD.PrintErr($"[WebViewSharp] WebView2 creation failed: {ex.Message}");
240:                 Cleanup();
241:             }
242:         }
243: 
244:         private void ConfigureWebView2()
245:         {
246:             if (_controller?.CoreWebView2 == null) return;
247: 
248:             var settings = _controller.CoreWebView2.Settings;
249: 
250:             // 应用导出属性配置
251:             settings.AreDefaultContextMenusEnabled = true;
252:             settings.IsStatusBarEnabled = true;
253:             settings.IsWebMessageEnabled = true;
254: 
255:             if (_host != null)
256:             {
257:                 settings.AreDevToolsEnabled = _host.DevToolsEnabled;
258:                 settings.IsZoomControlEnabled = _host.ZoomHotkeysEnabled;
259:                 settings.IsScriptEnabled = true;
260:             }
261: 
262:             _controller.IsVisible = true;
263:         }
264: 
265:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

267:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

269:         public void SetBounds(int x, int y, int width, int height)
270:         {
271:             if (_controller == null) return;
272: 
273:             // 更新 WebView2 控制器边界
274:             _controller.Bounds = new System.Drawing.Rectangle(x, y, width, height);
275: 
276:             // 同步子窗口位置
277:             if (_childHwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
278:             {
279:                 SetWindowPos(_childHwnd, HWND_TOP, x, y, width, height, SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
280:             }
281:         }

288:         {
289:             _controller?.CoreWebView2?.Navigate(url);
290:         }

293:         {
294:             _controller?.CoreWebView2?.NavigateToString(html);
295:         }

298:         {
299:             _controller?.CoreWebView2?.Reload();
300:         }

304:             _controller?.CoreWebView2?.GoBack();
305:         }

308:         {

310:         }

319:         }
320:

333:             ExecuteScript("if (window.__webviewDevTools) window.__webviewDevTools.close();");
334:         }

336:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

341:         {
342:             _controller?.CoreWebView2?.CallDevToolsProtocolMethodAsync("Storage.clearDataForOrigin",
343:                 "{\"origin\":\"*\",\"storageTypes\":\"all\"}");
344:         }

347:         {
348:             _controller?.MoveFocus(CoreWebView2MoveFocusReason.Programmatic);
349:         }

355:         }

357:         public void SetTransparent(bool transparent)
358:         {
359:             if (_controller == null) return;
360: 
361:             if (transparent)
362:             {
363:                 // WebView2 背景透明（参考 604Assembly WebView2Edge.cs）
364:                 _controller.DefaultBackgroundColor = System.Drawing.Color.Transparent;
365: 
366:                 // 开启 Godot 窗口透明，使 WebView 透明区域穿透显示 3D 场景
367:                 // 注意：必须在 WebView2 创建成功后调用，否则有冲突
368:                 try
369:                 {
370:                     DisplayServer.WindowSetFlag(DisplayServer.WindowFlags.Transparent, true, 0);
371:                     GD.Print("[WebViewSharp] Godot window transparent flag enabled");
372:                 }
373:                 catch (Exception ex)
374:                 {
375:                     GD.PrintErr($"[WebViewSharp] Failed to set window transparent: {ex.Message}");
376:                 }
377:             }
378:             else
379:             {
380:                 _controller.DefaultBackgroundColor = System.Drawing.Color.White;
381:             }
382:         }

388:         private void OnNavigationStarting(object sender, CoreWebView2NavigationStartingEventArgs e)
389:         {
390:             NavigationStarting?.Invoke(e.Uri);
391:         }
392: 
393:         private void OnNavigationCompleted(object sender, CoreWebView2NavigationCompletedEventArgs e)
394:         {

396:         }

399:         {
400:             SourceChanged?.Invoke(_controller?.CoreWebView2?.Source ?? "");
401:         }

404:         {
405:             string message = e.TryGetWebMessageAsString();
406:             if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
407:             {
408:                 WebMessageReceived?.Invoke(message);
409:             }
410:         }

416:         private IntPtr GetGodotWindowHandle()
417:         {
418:             try
419:             {
420:                 long handle = DisplayServer.WindowGetNativeHandle(DisplayServer.HandleType.WindowHandle, 0);
421:                 return new IntPtr(handle);
422:             }
423:             catch (Exception ex)
424:             {
425:                 GD.PrintErr($"[WebViewSharp] Failed to get window handle: {ex.Message}");
426:                 return IntPtr.Zero;
427:             }
428:         }

430:         private IntPtr FindWebView2ChildHwnd()
431:         {
432:             // WebView2 默认创建名为 "Chrome_WidgetWin_0" 的子窗口
433:             const string chromeWidgetClass = "Chrome_WidgetWin_0";
434:             IntPtr hwnd = FindWindowEx(_parentHwnd, IntPtr.Zero, chromeWidgetClass, null);
435: 
436:             if (hwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
437:             {
438:                 // 重试：有些 WebView2 版本使用不同的窗口类
439:                 hwnd = FindWindowEx(_parentHwnd, IntPtr.Zero, "Static", null);
440:             }
441: 
442:             return hwnd;
443:         }
444: 
445:         [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
446:         private static extern IntPtr FindWindowEx(IntPtr hWndParent, IntPtr hWndChildAfter, string lpszClass, string lpszWindow);
447: 
448:         private string GetUserDataDirectory()
449:         {
450:             if (_host != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_host.DataDirectory))
451:             {
452:                 string dir = _host.DataDirectory;
453:                 if (dir.StartsWith("user://"))
454:                 {
455:                     string path = dir.Substring(7).Replace("\\", "/");
456:                     string globalPath = ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath($"user://{path}");
457:                     Directory.CreateDirectory(globalPath);
458:                     return globalPath;
459:                 }
460:                 return dir;
461:             }
462: 
463:             string defaultDir = global::System.IO.Path.Combine(
464:                 global::System.Environment.GetFolderPath(global::System.Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData),
465:                 "GodotWebViewSharp"
466:             );
467:             Directory.CreateDirectory(defaultDir);
468:             return defaultDir;
469:         }
470: 
471:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
472:         //  清理 / 释放
473:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
474: 
475:         private void Cleanup()
476:         {
477:             if (_controller != null)
478:             {
479:                 if (_controller.CoreWebView2 != null)
480:                 {
481:                     _controller.CoreWebView2.NavigationStarting -= OnNavigationStarting;
482:                     _controller.CoreWebView2.NavigationCompleted -= OnNavigationCompleted;
483:                     _controller.CoreWebView2.SourceChanged -= OnSourceChanged;
484:                     _controller.CoreWebView2.WebMessageReceived -= OnWebMessageReceived;
485:                 }
486:                 _controller.Close();
487:                 _controller = null;
488:             }
489:             _environment = null;
490:             _childHwnd = IntPtr.Zero;
491:         }
492: 
493:         public void Dispose()

6: namespace WebViewSharp
7: {
8:     /// <summary>
9:     /// 输入事件转发。
10:     /// 将 WebView 中捕获的鼠标/键盘事件转发到 Godot 输入系统。
11:     ///
12:     /// 直接参考 godot_wry (lib.rs) 的 JS IPC input 事件处理逻辑：
13:     /// - _mouse_move → InputEventMouseMotion
14:     /// - _mouse_down/_mouse_up → InputEventMouseButton
15:     /// - _mouse_wheel → InputEventMouseButton (WHEEL_UP/WHEEL_DOWN)
16:     /// - _key_down/_key_up → InputEventKey
17:     /// </summary>
18:     internal static class InputEventRouter
19:     {
20:         private static MouseButtonMask _currentButtonMask = (MouseButtonMask)0;
21: 
22:         /// <summary>
23:         /// 尝试将 WebView IPC 消息作为输入事件处理。
24:         /// 返回 true 表示已处理（是输入事件），false 表示是普通 IPC 消息。
25:         /// </summary>
26:         public static bool TryProcessInputEvent(string json, Control target)
27:         {
28:             try
29:             {
30:                 var jsonObj = Json.ParseString(json);
31:                 if (jsonObj.VariantType != Variant.Type.Dictionary)
32:                     return false;
33: 
34:                 var dict = jsonObj.AsGodotDictionary();
35:                 if (!dict.ContainsKey("type"))
36:                     return false;
37: 
38:                 string type = dict["type"].AsString();
39:                 if (!type.StartsWith("_"))
40:                     return false;
41: 
42:                 var viewport = target.GetViewport();
43:                 if (viewport == null) return true; // 消费掉，但不是有效事件
44: 
45:                 float x = dict.GetValueOrDefault("x", 0.0f);
46:                 float y = dict.GetValueOrDefault("y", 0.0f);
47:                 var globalPos = target.GlobalPosition;
48:                 float vpX = globalPos.X + x;
49:                 float vpY = globalPos.Y + y;
50: 
51:                 switch (type)
52:                 {
53:                     case "_mouse_move":
54:                         return HandleMouseMove(dict, vpX, vpY, viewport);
55: 
56:                     case "_mouse_down":
57:                     case "_mouse_up":
58:                         return HandleMouseButton(type, dict, vpX, vpY, viewport);
59: 
60:                     case "_mouse_wheel":
61:                         return HandleMouseWheel(dict, vpX, vpY, viewport);
62: 
63:                     case "_key_down":
64:                     case "_key_up":
65:                         return HandleKeyboard(type, dict, viewport);
66: 
67:                     default:
68:                         return false;
69:                 }
70:             }
71:             catch
72:             {
73:                 return false;
74:             }
75:         }
76: 
77:         private static bool HandleMouseMove(Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)
78:         {
79:             float movementX = dict.GetValueOrDefault("movementX", 0.0f);
80:             float movementY = dict.GetValueOrDefault("movementY", 0.0f);
81: 
82:             var ev = new InputEventMouseMotion
83:             {
84:                 Position = new Vector2(vpX, vpY),
85:                 GlobalPosition = new Vector2(vpX, vpY),
86:                 ButtonMask = _currentButtonMask,
87:                 Relative = new Vector2(movementX, movementY)
88:             };
89:             viewport.PushInput(ev);
90:             return true;
91:         }
92: 
93:         private static bool HandleMouseButton(string type, Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)
94:         {
95:             int button = dict.GetValueOrDefault("button", 0);
96:             var godotButton = MapMouseButton(button);
97:             bool pressed = type == "_mouse_down";
98: 
99:             // 更新按钮掩码
100:             UpdateButtonMask(godotButton, pressed);
101: 
102:             var ev = new InputEventMouseButton
103:             {
104:                 ButtonIndex = godotButton,
105:                 Position = new Vector2(vpX, vpY),
106:                 GlobalPosition = new Vector2(vpX, vpY),
107:                 Pressed = pressed,
108:                 ButtonMask = _currentButtonMask
109:             };
110:             viewport.PushInput(ev);
111:             return true;
112:         }
113: 
114:         private static bool HandleMouseWheel(Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)
115:         {
116:             float deltaX = dict.GetValueOrDefault("deltaX", 0.0f);
117:             float deltaY = dict.GetValueOrDefault("deltaY", 0.0f);
118:             var position = new Vector2(vpX, vpY);
119: 
120:             if (deltaY != 0.0f)
121:             {
122:                 var button = deltaY < 0.0f ? MouseButton.WheelUp : MouseButton.WheelDown;
123:                 float factor = Math.Max(Math.Abs(deltaY) / 100.0f, 1.0f);
124:                 SendWheelEvent(button, position, factor, viewport);
125:             }
126: 
127:             if (deltaX != 0.0f)
128:             {
129:                 var button = deltaX < 0.0f ? MouseButton.WheelLeft : MouseButton.WheelRight;
130:                 float factor = Math.Max(Math.Abs(deltaX) / 100.0f, 1.0f);
131:                 SendWheelEvent(button, position, factor, viewport);
132:             }
133: 
134:             return true;
135:         }
136: 
137:         private static void SendWheelEvent(MouseButton button, Vector2 position, float factor, Viewport viewport)
138:         {
139:             foreach (bool pressed in new[] { true, false })
140:             {
141:                 var ev = new InputEventMouseButton
142:                 {
143:                     ButtonIndex = button,
144:                     Position = position,
145:                     GlobalPosition = position,
146:                     Pressed = pressed,
147:                     Factor = factor,
148:                     ButtonMask = _currentButtonMask
149:                 };
150:                 viewport.PushInput(ev);
151:             }
152:         }
153: 
154:         private static bool HandleKeyboard(string type, Dictionary dict, Viewport viewport)
155:         {
156:             string keyStr = dict.GetValueOrDefault("key", "");
157:             var godotKey = MapGodotKey(keyStr);
158: 
159:             var ev = new InputEventKey
160:             {
161:                 Keycode = godotKey,
162:                 Pressed = type == "_key_down",
163:                 ShiftPressed = dict.GetValueOrDefault("shift", false),
164:                 CtrlPressed = dict.GetValueOrDefault("ctrl", false),
165:                 AltPressed = dict.GetValueOrDefault("alt", false),
166:                 MetaPressed = dict.GetValueOrDefault("meta", false)
167:             };
168:             viewport.PushInput(ev);
169:             return true;
170:         }
171:

176:         private static Key MapGodotKey(string key)

214:                 "f10" => Key.F10, "f11" => Key.F11, "f12" => Key.F12,
215: 
216:                 "arrowup" => Key.Up, "arrowdown" => Key.Down,

218:

220:                 "space" or " " => Key.Space,
221:                 "backspace" => Key.Backspace, "escape" => Key.Escape,
222:                 "capslock" => Key.Capslock, "scrolllock" => Key.Scrolllock,
223:                 "numlock" => Key.Numlock, "printscreen" => Key.Print,
224:                 "pause" => Key.Pause, "insert" => Key.Insert,
225:                 "home" => Key.Home, "pageup" => Key.Pageup,
226:                 "delete" => Key.Delete, "end" => Key.End,
227:                 "pagedown" => Key.Pagedown,
228: 
229:                 "shift" => Key.Shift, "control" => Key.Ctrl,
230:                 "alt" => Key.Alt, "meta" => Key.Meta,
231:                 "contextmenu" => Key.Menu,
232: 
233:                 _ => Key.None
234:             };
235:         }
236: 
237:         private static MouseButton MapMouseButton(int button)
238:         {
239:             return button switch
240:             {
241:                 0 => MouseButton.Left,
242:                 1 => MouseButton.Middle,
243:                 2 => MouseButton.Right,
244:                 3 => MouseButton.WheelUp,
245:                 4 => MouseButton.WheelDown,
246:                 _ => MouseButton.Left
247:             };
248:         }
249: 
250:         private static void UpdateButtonMask(MouseButton button, bool pressed)
251:         {
252:             int maskInt = button switch
253:             {
254:                 MouseButton.Left => 1,     // MouseButtonMask.Left
255:                 MouseButton.Right => 2,    // MouseButtonMask.Right
256:                 MouseButton.Middle => 4,   // MouseButtonMask.Middle
257:                 _ => -1
258:             };
259: 
260:             if (maskInt == -1) return;
261:             int current = (int)(object)_currentButtonMask;
262: 
263:             if (pressed)
264:                 current |= maskInt;
265:             else
266:                 current &= ~maskInt;
267: 
268:             _currentButtonMask = (MouseButtonMask)(object)current;
269:         }
270: 
271:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
272:         //  Dictionary 扩展
273:         // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
274: 
275:         private static T GetValueOrDefault<T>(this Dictionary dict, string key, T defaultValue)
276:         {
277:             if (dict.ContainsKey(key))
278:             {
279:                 var val = dict[key];
280:                 if (val.VariantType != Variant.Type.Nil)
281:                 {
282:                     return (T)val.Obj;
283:                 }
284:             }
285:             return defaultValue;
286:         }
287:     }
288: }

Now I have a complete understanding of the GDCefGlue extension project structure. Let me also look at the rest of the extension files to understand the full pattern.

The GDExtension project structure:
- `Main.cs` - Entry point with `[UnmanagedCallersOnly]` for the native init
- `CefGlueControl.cs` - The main control class (skeleton)
- Multiple `CefGlueControl.*.cs` partial files for different concerns
- `CefInitializer.cs` - CEF initialization
- Various handler files
- `Platform/` - Platform-specific native methods

The key differences between a normal Godot C# plugin and a GDExtension:
1. Uses `Microsoft.NET.Sdk` instead of `Godot.NET.Sdk`
2. References `Godot.Bindings.dll` directly (compiled from godot-dotnet)
3. Uses `PublishAot=true` for native AOT compilation
4. Entry point via `[UnmanagedCallersOnly]` instead of the normal Godot entry
5. Uses `GodotBridge` and `GodotRegistry` for registration

Now, the user's current project `GodotWebViewSharp` uses WebView2/WKWebView/WebKitGTK (system webviews). The GDExtension version would:
1. Use the same system webview backends
2. But with godot-dotnet bindings instead of Godot.NET.Sdk
3. Compile as native AOT GDExtension

Let me also look at the remaining extension source files to understand the full pattern.

54:             }
55: 
56:             GD.Print($"CefGlueControl: Godot HWND = 0x{_godotHwnd.ToInt64():X8}");
57:             windowInfo.SetAsChild(_godotHwnd, new CefRectangle(0, 0, width, height));
58:         }
59:         else
60:         {
61:             windowInfo.SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent);
62:         }
63:         var settings = new CefBrowserSettings { WindowlessFrameRate = frameRate };
64:         _client = new GodotCefClient(this);
65:         try { CefBrowserHost.CreateBrowser(windowInfo, _client, settings, InitialUrl); }
66:         catch (Exception ex) { GD.PrintErr($"CefGlueControl: Failed to create browser - {ex.Message}"); }
67:     }
68: 
69:     internal void OnBrowserCreated(CefBrowser browser)
70:     {
71:         if (_browser != null) return;
72:         _browser = browser; _browserHost = browser.GetHost();
73: 
74:         if (_renderMode == RenderMode.EmbeddedWindow && _browserHost != null)
75:         {
76:             _cefChildHwnd = _browserHost.GetWindowHandle();
77:             if (_cefChildHwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
78:                 GD.Print($"CefGlueControl: CEF child HWND = 0x{_cefChildHwnd.ToInt64():X8}");
79:             else
80:                 GD.Print("CefGlueControl: GetWindowHandle returned zero, will retry in _Process");
81:         }
82: 
83:         CallDeferred("_notify_browser_initialized");
84:     }
85: 
86:     private void NotifyBrowserInitialized()
87:     {
88:         RegisterEventForwarder();
89:         BrowserInitialized?.Invoke();
90:         EmitSignal(new StringName(nameof(BrowserInitialized)));
91:         GD.Print("CefGlueControl: Browser initialized");
92:     }
93: }

(End of file - total 93 lines)
</content>

16:     public void ShowDeveloperTools() { var w = CefWindowInfo.Create(); w.RuntimeStyle = CefRuntimeStyle.Chrome; _browserHost?.ShowDevTools(w, _client, new CefBrowserSettings(), new CefPoint()); }
17:     public void CloseDeveloperTools() => _browserHost?.CloseDevTools();
18: 
19:     public void EvalJs(string code) => _ = EvalJsAsync(code);
20:     private async Task EvalJsAsync(string code)
21:     { string result = null, error = null; try { result = await InternalEvalRaw($"return {code};"); } catch (Exception ex) { error = ex.Message; } CallDeferred(nameof(OnEvalDone), result ?? "", error ?? ""); }
22:     private Task<string> InternalEvalRaw(string code)
23:     { var frame = _browser?.GetMainFrame(); if (frame == null) return Task.FromResult<string>(null); var id = Interlocked.Increment(ref _lastEvalTaskId); var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<string>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously); _pendingEvals.TryAdd(id, tcs); var m = CefProcessMessage.Create("JsEvaluationRequest"); using (var a = m.Arguments) { a.SetInt(0, id); a.SetString(1, code); a.SetString(2, "about:blank"); a.SetInt(3, 1); } frame.SendProcessMessage(CefProcessId.Renderer, m); return tcs.Task; }
24:     private void OnEvalDone(string result, string error) => EmitSignal(new StringName("eval_completed"), result, error);
25: 
26:     // ── CEF 事件回调 ──
27:     internal void OnAddressChange(CefBrowser browser, CefFrame frame, string url)
28:     { if (frame.IsMain) CallDeferred("_notify_address_changed", url); }
29:     private void _notify_address_changed(string url) => AddressChanged?.Invoke(this, url);
30: 
31:     internal void OnTitleChange(CefBrowser browser, string title)
32:     { Title = title; CallDeferred("_notify_title_changed", title); }
33:     private void _notify_title_changed(string title) => TitleChanged?.Invoke(this, title);
34: 
35:     internal void OnLoadStart(CefBrowser browser, CefFrame frame, CefTransitionType transitionType)
36:     => CallDeferred("_notify_load_start");
37:     private void _notify_load_start() { LoadStart?.Invoke(this, new LoadStartEventArgs(null)); EmitSignal(new StringName(nameof(LoadStart))); }
38: 
39:     internal void OnLoadEnd(CefBrowser browser, CefFrame frame, int httpStatusCode)
40:     {
41:         // 每次页面加载后重新注册 JS handler（BrowserProcess的V8上下文重建不可靠）
42:         if (frame.IsMain)
43:         {
44:             foreach (var kv in _jsHandlerMethods)
45:             {
46:                 var msg = CefProcessMessage.Create("NativeObjectRegistrationRequest");
47:                 using (var a = msg.Arguments) { a.SetString(0, kv.Key); a.SetString(1, kv.Value); }
48:                 frame.SendProcessMessage(CefProcessId.Renderer, msg);
49:             }
50:             // 嵌入模式下注入事件转发 JS
51:             InjectEventForwardingScriptIfNeeded();
52:         }
53:         CallDeferred("_notify_load_end");
54:     }
55:     private void _notify_load_end() { LoadEnd?.Invoke(this, new LoadEndEventArgs(null, 0)); EmitSignal(new StringName(nameof(LoadEnd))); }
56: 
57:     internal void OnLoadError(CefBrowser browser, CefFrame frame, CefErrorCode errorCode, string errorText, string failedUrl)
58:     { CallDeferred("_notify_load_error", errorText, failedUrl); }
59:     private void _notify_load_error(string errorText, string failedUrl)
60:     { LoadError?.Invoke(this, new LoadErrorEventArgs(null, CefErrorCode.None, errorText, failedUrl)); EmitSignal(new StringName(nameof(LoadError)), errorText, failedUrl); }
61: }

7:     /// <summary>
8:     /// 跨平台原生窗口操作方法。

12:     {
13:         /// <summary>
14:         /// 移动并调整窗口位置和大小。
15:         /// Win32: SetWindowPos | macOS: setFrame: | Linux: XMoveResizeWindow
16:         /// </summary>
17:         internal static void MovePlatformWindow(IntPtr handle, int x, int y, int width, int height)
18:         {
19:             if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
20:                 MacSetViewFrame(handle, x, y, width, height);
21:             else if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux())
22:                 LinuxMoveResizeWindow(handle, x, y, width, height);
23:             else
24:                 Win32SetWindowPos(handle, HWND_TOP, x, y, width, height,
25:                     SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
26:         }
27: 
28:         /// <summary>
29:         /// 将键盘焦点设置到指定窗口。
30:         /// Win32: SetFocus | macOS: makeFirstResponder | Linux: XSetInputFocus
31:         /// </summary>
32:         internal static void SetPlatformFocus(IntPtr window)
33:         {
34:             if (OperatingSystem.IsLinux())
35:                 LinuxSetInputFocus(window);
36:             else if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
37:                 MacMakeFirstResponder(window);
38:             else
39:                 Win32SetFocus(window);

70:         private static void MacSetViewFrame(IntPtr nsView, int x, int y, int width, int height)
71:         {
72:             // [nsView setFrame:NSMakeRect(x, y, width, height)]
73:             IntPtr sel = sel_registerName("setFrame:");
74:             // NSRect 在 macOS 64-bit 上是 32 bytes (4 doubles)
75:             byte[] rect = new byte[32];
76:             BitConverter.GetBytes((double)x).CopyTo(rect, 0);
77:             BitConverter.GetBytes((double)y).CopyTo(rect, 8);
78:             BitConverter.GetBytes((double)width).CopyTo(rect, 16);
79:             BitConverter.GetBytes((double)height).CopyTo(rect, 24);
80:             objc_msgSend(nsView, sel);
81:         }

═══════════════════════════════════════════════\n    //  初始化 / 清理\n    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n    private async Task InitializeWebViewAsync()\n    {\n        try\n        {\n            // 平台选择\n            if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())\n            {\n#if MACOS\n                _webview = new MacWebViewBackend();\n#else\n                GD.PrintErr(\"[GDEWebView] += OnNavigationStarting;\n            _webview.NavigationCompleted += OnNavigationCompleted;\n            _webview.WebMessageReceived += OnWebMessageReceived;\n            _webview.SourceChanged += _webview.WebMessageReceived -= OnWebMessageReceived;\n            _webview.SourceChanged -= OnSourceChanged;\n\n            _webview.Dispose();\n            _webview = null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════\n    //  位置同步\n    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n    private void SyncPosition()\n    {\n        if (_webview == null || !_webview.IsCreated)\n            return;\n\n        var globalPos = GlobalPosition;\n        var size = Size;\n        if (size.X <= 0 || size.Y <= 0)\n            return;\n\n        float contentScale = DisplayServer.Singleton.ScreenGetScale();\n        var windowPos = DisplayServer.Singleton.WindowGetPosition();\n\n        // 仅在有变化时更新\n        if (globalPos == _previousGlobalPos\n
